{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c6b57df1a12d4b612ca4ab1dad19e0","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_c2hoqad87siq_1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/mars_clustering_optimization.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nMars Cloud Clustering Optimization\n\nThis script optimizes DBSCAN hyperparameters to cluster citizen science\nannotations of Mars clouds and finds the Pareto frontier of solutions\nthat balance F1 score and delta.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom sklearn.cluster import DBSCAN\nfrom sklearn.metrics import pairwise_distances\nfrom itertools import product\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n\ndef shape_weighted_distance(a, b, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute custom shape-weighted distance between two points.\n\n    Args:\n        a, b: Points as (x, y) tuples\n        shape_weight: Weight parameter w (0.9-1.9)\n\n    Returns:\n        Euclidean distance with shape weighting\n    \"\"\"\n    dx = a[0] - b[0]\n    dy = a[1] - b[1]\n    return np.sqrt((shape_weight * dx)**2 + ((2 - shape_weight) * dy)**2)\n\n\ndef custom_dbscan(X, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run DBSCAN with custom distance metric.\n\n    Args:\n        X: Array of points (n_samples, 2)\n        epsilon: Maximum distance between samples\n        min_samples: Minimum samples in neighborhood\n        shape_weight: Shape weight parameter\n\n    Returns:\n        Cluster labels\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(X) == 0:\n        return np.array([])\n\n    # Compute pairwise distances using custom metric\n    n = len(X)\n    distance_matrix = np.zeros((n, n))\n\n    for i in range(n):\n        for j in range(i+1, n):\n            dist = shape_weighted_distance(X[i], X[j], shape_weight)\n            distance_matrix[i, j] = dist\n            distance_matrix[j, i] = dist\n\n    # Use DBSCAN with precomputed distance matrix\n    dbscan = DBSCAN(eps=epsilon, min_samples=min_samples, metric='precomputed')\n    labels = dbscan.fit_predict(distance_matrix)\n\n    return labels\n\n\ndef compute_cluster_centroids(X, labels):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute centroids for each cluster (excluding noise points with label -1).\n\n    Args:\n        X: Array of points\n        labels: Cluster labels\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping cluster_id to centroid (x, y)\n    \"\"\"\n    centroids = {}\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(labels):\n        if cluster_id != -1:  # Exclude noise points\n            cluster_points = X[labels == cluster_id]\n            centroid = np.mean(cluster_points, axis=0)\n            centroids[cluster_id] = (centroid[0], centroid[1])\n\n    return centroids\n\n\ndef greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points, max_distance=100):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match cluster centroids to expert annotations using greedy matching.\n\n    Args:\n        cluster_centroids: List of cluster centroid tuples\n        expert_points: List of expert point tuples\n        max_distance: Maximum allowed matching distance\n\n    Returns:\n        List of tuples (centroid_idx, expert_idx, distance)\n    \"\"\"\n    matches = []\n    used_experts = set()\n\n    # Create list of all possible pairs with distances\n    all_pairs = []\n    for i, centroid in enumerate(cluster_centroids):\n        for j, expert in enumerate(expert_points):\n            dist = np.sqrt((centroid[0] - expert[0])**2 + (centroid[1] - expert[1])**2)\n            if dist <= max_distance:\n                all_pairs.append((dist, i, j))\n\n    # Sort by distance and greedily match\n    all_pairs.sort()\n    for dist, i, j in all_pairs:\n        if j not in used_experts:\n            matches.append((i, j, dist))\n            used_experts.add(j)\n\n    return matches\n\n\ndef compute_f1_score(num_matches, num_clusters, num_experts):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute F1 score based on matches, clusters, and experts.\n\n    Args:\n        num_matches: Number of matched pairs\n        num_clusters: Number of clusters found\n        num_experts: Number of expert annotations\n\n    Returns:\n        F1 score\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_clusters == 0 and num_experts == 0:\n        return 1.0\n    if num_clusters == 0 or num_experts == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    precision = num_matches / num_clusters if num_clusters > 0 else 0\n    recall = num_matches / num_experts if num_experts > 0 else 0\n\n    if precision + recall == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    f1 = 2 * (precision * recall) / (precision + recall)\n    return f1\n\n\ndef evaluate_image(citsci_points, expert_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate clustering performance for a single image.\n\n    Args:\n        citsci_points: Citizen science points for the image\n        expert_points: Expert annotations for the image\n        epsilon: DBSCAN epsilon parameter\n        min_samples: DBSCAN min_samples parameter\n        shape_weight: Shape weight parameter\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple (f1_score, delta)\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(citsci_points) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    # Run DBSCAN\n    labels = custom_dbscan(citsci_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight)\n\n    # Compute cluster centroids\n    centroids = compute_cluster_centroids(citsci_points, labels)\n    cluster_centroids = list(centroids.values())\n    expert_points_list = [(p[0], p[1]) for p in expert_points]\n\n    # Match clusters to experts\n    matches = greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points_list)\n\n    # Compute F1 score\n    f1 = compute_f1_score(len(matches), len(cluster_centroids), len(expert_points_list))\n\n    # Compute delta (average distance of matches)\n    if len(matches) > 0:\n        delta = np.mean([match[2] for match in matches])\n    else:\n        delta = np.nan\n\n    return f1, delta\n\n\ndef pareto_frontier(points):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find Pareto frontier from a list of points.\n\n    Args:\n        points: List of tuples (f1, delta, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of Pareto-optimal points\n    \"\"\"\n    # Sort by F1 (descending) then by delta (ascending)\n    sorted_points = sorted(points, key=lambda p: (-p[0], p[1]))\n\n    pareto_points = []\n    min_delta = float('inf')\n\n    for point in sorted_points:\n        f1, delta = point[0], point[1]\n\n        # If this point has a lower delta than any seen before at this F1 level\n        # (accounting for floating point precision)\n        if delta < min_delta - 1e-10:\n            pareto_points.append(point)\n            min_delta = delta\n\n    return pareto_points\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n\n    # Load datasets\n    citsci_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/citsci_train.csv')\n    expert_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/expert_train.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(citsci_df)} citizen science annotations\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(expert_df)} expert annotations\")\n\n    # Extract relevant columns and remove NaN values\n    citsci_df = citsci_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].dropna()\n    expert_df = expert_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].dropna()\n\n    # Get unique images from expert dataset\n    unique_images = expert_df['file_rad'].unique()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_images)} unique images in expert dataset\")\n\n    # Group by image\n    citsci_grouped = citsci_df.groupby('file_rad')\n    expert_grouped = expert_df.groupby('file_rad')\n\n    # Hyperparameter search space\n    min_samples_range = range(3, 10)  # 3-9\n    epsilon_range = range(4, 25, 2)  # 4, 6, 8, ..., 22, 24\n    shape_weight_range = np.arange(0.9, 2.0, 0.1)  # 0.9, 1.0, 1.1, ..., 1.8, 1.9\n\n    total_combinations = len(min_samples_range) * len(epsilon_range) * len(shape_weight_range)\n    print(f\"\\nTotal hyperparameter combinations: {total_combinations}\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Grid search\n    for min_samples in min_samples_range:\n        for epsilon in epsilon_range:\n            for shape_weight in shape_weight_range:\n                f1_scores = []\n                deltas = []\n\n                # Evaluate each image\n                for image_id in unique_images:\n                    # Get citizen science points for this image\n                    if image_id in citsci_grouped.groups:\n                        citsci_points = citsci_grouped.get_group(image_id)[['x', 'y']].values\n                    else:\n                        citsci_points = np.array([])\n\n                    # Get expert points for this image\n                    expert_points = expert_grouped.get_group(image_id)[['x', 'y']].values\n\n                    # Evaluate\n                    f1, delta = evaluate_image(\n                        citsci_points, expert_points,\n                        epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight\n                    )\n\n                    f1_scores.append(f1)\n                    deltas.append(delta)\n\n                # Compute averages\n                avg_f1 = np.mean(f1_scores)\n\n                # Only include results with F1 > 0.5\n                if avg_f1 > 0.5:\n                    # Average delta (excluding NaN values)\n                    valid_deltas = [d for d in deltas if not np.isnan(d)]\n                    avg_delta = np.mean(valid_deltas) if valid_deltas else np.nan\n\n                    results.append({\n                        'F1': avg_f1,\n                        'delta': avg_delta,\n                        'min_samples': min_samples,\n                        'epsilon': epsilon,\n                        'shape_weight': shape_weight\n                    })\n\n                    print(f\"min_samples={min_samples}, epsilon={epsilon}, shape_weight={shape_weight:.1f}: \"\n                          f\"F1={avg_f1:.5f}, delta={avg_delta:.5f}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nFiltered results (F1 > 0.5): {len(results)}\")\n\n    # Find Pareto frontier\n    if results:\n        # Convert to list of tuples\n        points = [(r['F1'], r['delta'], r['min_samples'], r['epsilon'], r['shape_weight'])\n                  for r in results]\n\n        pareto_points = pareto_frontier(points)\n\n        print(f\"Pareto frontier points: {len(pareto_points)}\")\n\n        # Prepare output\n        output_data = []\n        for f1, delta, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight in pareto_points:\n            output_data.append({\n                'F1': round(f1, 5),\n                'delta': round(delta, 5),\n                'min_samples': int(min_samples),\n                'epsilon': int(epsilon),\n                'shape_weight': round(shape_weight, 1)\n            })\n\n        # Sort by F1 descending\n        output_data.sort(key=lambda x: (-x['F1'], x['delta']))\n\n        # Write to CSV\n        output_df = pd.DataFrame(output_data)\n        output_df.to_csv('/root/pareto_frontier.csv', index=False)\n\n        print(f\"\\nResults written to /root/pareto_frontier.csv\")\n        print(\"\\nPareto frontier:\")\n        print(output_df.to_string(index=False))\n    else:\n        print(\"No results with F1 > 0.5 found!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":144,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":610,"cache_read_input_tokens":31188},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"60e82386-911a-49c3-a32c-356910fd636e","uuid":"b66f8d21-a012-4f3e-989f-4c95d4be42f7"}

Sort by distance and greedily match\n    all_pairs.sort()\n    for dist, i, j in all_pairs:\n        if j not in used_experts:\n            matches.append((i, j, dist))\n            used_experts.add(j)\n\n    return matches\n\n\ndef compute_f1_score(num_matches, num_clusters, num_experts):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute F1 score based on matches, clusters, and experts.\n\n    Args:\n        num_matches: Number of matched pairs\n        num_clusters: Number of clusters found\n        num_experts: Number of expert annotations\n\n    Returns:\n        F1 score\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_clusters == 0 and num_experts == 0:\n        return 1.0\n    if num_clusters == 0 or num_experts == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    precision = num_matches / num_clusters if num_clusters > 0 else 0\n    recall = num_matches / num_experts if num_experts > 0 else 0\n\n    if precision + recall == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    f1 = 2 * (precision * recall) / (precision + recall)\n    return f1\n\n\ndef evaluate_image(citsci_points, expert_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate clustering performance for a single image.\n\n    Args:\n        citsci_points: Citizen science points for the image\n        expert_points: Expert annotations for the image\n        epsilon: DBSCAN epsilon parameter\n        min_samples: DBSCAN min_samples parameter\n        shape_weight: Shape weight parameter\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple (f1_score, delta)\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(citsci_points) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    # Run DBSCAN\n    labels = custom_dbscan(citsci_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight)\n\n    # Compute cluster centroids\n    centroids = compute_cluster_centroids(citsci_points, labels)\n    cluster_centroids = list(centroids.values())\n    expert_points_list = [(p[0], p[1]) for p in expert_points]\n\n    # Match clusters to experts\n    matches = greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points_list)\n\n    # Compute F1 score\n    f1 = compute_f1_score(len(matches), len(cluster_centroids), len(expert_points_list))\n\n    # Compute delta (average distance of matches)\n    if len(matches) > 0:\n        delta = np.mean([match[2] for match in matches])\n    else:\n        delta = np.nan\n\n    return f1, delta\n\n\ndef pareto_frontier(points):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find Pareto frontier from a list of points.\n\n    Args:\n        points: List of tuples (f1, delta, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of Pareto-optimal points\n    \"\"\"\n    # Sort by F1 (descending) then by delta (ascending)\n    sorted_points = sorted(points, key=lambda p: (-p[0], p[1]))\n\n    pareto_points = []\n    min_delta = float('inf')\n\n    for point in sorted_points:\n        f1, delta = point[0], point[1]\n\n        # If this point has a lower delta than any seen before at this F1 level\n        # (accounting for floating point precision)\n        if delta < min_delta - 1e-10:\n            pareto_points.append(point)\n            min_delta = delta\n\n    return pareto_points\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n\n    # Load datasets\n    citsci_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/citsci_train.csv')\n    expert_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/expert_train.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(citsci_df)} citizen science annotations\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(expert_df)} expert annotations\")\n\n    # Extract relevant columns and remove NaN values\n    citsci_df = citsci_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].dropna()\n    expert_df = expert_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].dropna()\n\n    # Get unique images from expert dataset\n    unique_images = expert_df['file_rad'].unique()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_images)} unique images in expert dataset\")\n\n    # Group by image\n    citsci_grouped = citsci_df.groupby('file_rad')\n    expert_grouped = expert_df.groupby('file_rad')\n\n    # Hyperparameter search space\n    min_samples_range = range(3, 10)  # 3-9\n    epsilon_range = range(4, 25, 2)  # 4, 6, 8, ..., 22, 24\n    shape_weight_range = np.arange(0.9, 2.0, 0.1)  # 0.9, 1.0, 1.1, ..., 1.8, 1.9\n\n    total_combinations = len(min_samples_range) * len(epsilon_range) * len(shape_weight_range)\n    print(f\"\\nTotal hyperparameter combinations: {total_combinations}\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Grid search\n    for min_samples in min_samples_range:\n        for epsilon in epsilon_range:\n            for shape_weight in shape_weight_range:\n                f1_scores = []\n                deltas = []\n\n                # Evaluate each image\n                for image_id in unique_images:\n                    # Get citizen science points for this image\n                    if image_id in citsci_grouped.groups:\n                        citsci_points = citsci_grouped.get_group(image_id)[['x', 'y']].values\n                    else:\n                        citsci_points = np.array([])\n\n                    # Get expert points for this image\n                    expert_points = expert_grouped.get_group(image_id)[['x', 'y']].values\n\n                    # Evaluate\n                    f1, delta = evaluate_image(\n                        citsci_points, expert_points,\n                        epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight\n                    )\n\n                    f1_scores.append(f1)\n                    deltas.append(delta)\n\n                # Compute averages\n                avg_f1 = np.mean(f1_scores)\n\n                # Only include results with F1 > 0.5\n                if avg_f1 > 0.5:\n                    # Average delta (excluding NaN values)\n                    valid_deltas = [d for d in deltas if not np.isnan(d)]\n                    avg_delta = np.mean(valid_deltas) if valid_deltas else np.nan\n\n                    results.append({\n                        'F1': avg_f1,\n                        'delta': avg_delta,\n                        'min_samples': min_samples,\n                        'epsilon': epsilon,\n                        'shape_weight': shape_weight\n                    })\n\n                    print(f\"min_samples={min_samples}, epsilon={epsilon}, shape_weight={shape_weight:.1f}: \"\n                          f\"F1={avg_f1:.5f}, delta={avg_delta:.5f}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nFiltered results (F1 > 0.5): {len(results)}\")\n\n    # Find Pareto frontier\n    if results:\n        # Convert to list of tuples\n        points = [(r['F1'], r['delta'], r['min_samples'], r['epsilon'], r['shape_weight'])\n                  for r in results]\n\n        pareto_points = pareto_frontier(points)\n\n        print(f\"Pareto frontier points: {len(pareto_points)}\")\n\n        # Prepare output\n        output_data = []\n        for f1, delta, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight in pareto_points:\n            output_data.append({\n                'F1': round(f1, 5),\n                'delta': round(delta, 5),\n                'min_samples': int(min_samples),\n                'epsilon': int(epsilon),\n                'shape_weight': round(shape_weight, 1)\n            })\n\n        # Sort by F1 descending\n        output_data.sort(key=lambda x: (-x['F1'], x['delta']))\n\n        # Write to CSV\n        output_df = pd.DataFrame(output_data)\n        output_df.to_csv('/root/pareto_frontier.csv', index=False)\n\n        print(f\"\\nResults written to /root/pareto_frontier.csv\")\n        print(\"\\nPareto frontier:\")\n        print(output_df.to_string(index=False))\n    else:\n        print(\"No results with F1 > 0.5 found!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

2)\n        epsilon: Maximum distance between samples\n        min_samples: Minimum samples in neighborhood\n        shape_weight: Shape weight parameter\n\n    Returns:\n        Cluster labels\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(X) == 0:\n        return np.array([])\n\n    # Compute pairwise distances using custom metric\n    n = len(X)\n    distance_matrix = np.zeros((n, n))\n\n    for i in range(n):\n Compute pairwise distances using custom metric\n    n = len(X)\n    distance_matrix = np.zeros((n, n))\n\n    for i in range(n):\n        for j in range(i+1, n):\n            dist = shape_weighted_distance(X[i], X[j], shape_weight)\n            distance_matrix[i, j] = dist\n            distance_matrix[j, i] = dist\n\n    # Use DBSCAN with precomputed distance matrix\n    dbscan = DBSCAN(eps=epsilon, min_samples=min_samples, metric='precomputed')\n    labels = dbscan.fit_predict(distance_matrix)\n\n    return labels\n\n\ndef compute_cluster_centroids(X, labels):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute centroids for each cluster (excluding noise points with label -1).\n\n    Args:\n        X: Array of points\n        labels: Cluster labels\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping cluster_id to centroid (x, y)\n    \"\"\"\n    centroids = {}\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(labels):\n        if cluster_id != -1:  # Exclude noise points\n            cluster_points = X[labels == cluster_id]\n            centroid = np.mean(cluster_points, axis=0)\n            centroids[cluster_id] = (centroid[0], centroid[1])\n\n    return centroids\n\n\ndef greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points, max_distance=100):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match cluster centroids to expert annotations using greedy matching.\n\n    Args:\n        cluster_centroids: List of cluster centroid tuples\n        expert_points: List of expert point tuples\n        max_distance: Maximum allowed matching distance\n\n    Returns:\n        List of tuples (centroid_idx, expert_idx, distance)\n    \"\"\"\n    matches = []\n    used_experts = set()\n\n    # Create list of all possible pairs with distances\n    all_pairs = []\n    for i, centroid in enumerate(cluster_centroids):\n        for j, expert in enumerate(expert_points):\n            dist = np.sqrt((centroid[0] - expert[0])**2 + (centroid[1] - expert[1])**2)\n            if dist <= max_distance:\n                all_pairs.append((dist, i, j))\n\n    # Sort by distance and greedily match\n    all_pairs.sort()\n    for dist, i, j in all_pairs:\n        if j not in used_experts:\n            matches.append((i, j, dist))\n            used_experts.add(j)\n\n    return matches\n\n\ndef compute_f1_score(num_matches, num_clusters, num_experts):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute F1 score based on matches, clusters, and experts.\n\n    Args:\n        num_matches: Number of matched pairs\n        num_clusters: Number of clusters found\n        num_experts: Number of expert annotations\n\n    Returns:\n        F1 score\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_clusters == 0 and num_experts == 0:\n        return 1.0\n    if num_clusters == 0 or num_experts == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    precision = num_matches / num_clusters if num_clusters > 0 else 0\n    recall = num_matches / num_experts if num_experts > 0 else 0\n\n    if precision + recall == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    f1 = 2 * (precision * recall) / (precision + recall)\n    return f1\n\n\ndef evaluate_image(citsci_points, expert_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate clustering performance for a single image.\n\n    Args:\n        citsci_points: Citizen science points for the image\n        expert_points: Expert annotations for the image\n        epsilon: DBSCAN epsilon parameter\n        min_samples: DBSCAN min_samples parameter\n        shape_weight: Shape weight parameter\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple (f1_score, delta)\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(citsci_points) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    # Run DBSCAN\n    labels = custom_dbscan(citsci_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight)\n\n    # Compute cluster centroids\n    centroids = compute_cluster_centroids(citsci_points, labels)\n    cluster_centroids = list(centroids.values())\n    expert_points_list = [(p[0], p[1]) for p in expert_points]\n\n    # Match clusters to experts\n    matches = greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points_list)\n\n    # Compute F1 score\n    f1 = compute_f1_score(len(matches), len(cluster_centroids), len(expert_points_list))\n\n    # Compute delta (average distance of matches)\n    if len(matches) > 0:\n        delta = np.mean([match[2] for match in matches])\n    else:\n        delta = np.nan\n\n    return f1, delta\n\n\ndef pareto_frontier(points):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find Pareto frontier from a list of points.\n\n    Args:\n        points: List of tuples (f1, delta, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of Pareto-optimal points\n    \"\"\"\n    # Sort by F1 (descending) then by delta (ascending)\n    sorted_points = sorted(points, key=lambda p: (-p[0], p[1]))\n\n    pareto_points = []\n    min_delta = float('inf')\n\n    for point in sorted_points:\n        f1, delta = point[0], point[1]\n\n        # If this point has a lower delta than any seen before at this F1 level\n        # (accounting for floating point precision)\n        if delta < min_delta - 1e-10:\n            pareto_points.append(point)\n            min_delta = delta\n\n    return pareto_points\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n\n    # Load datasets\n    citsci_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/citsci_train.csv')\n    expert_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/expert_train.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(citsci_df)} citizen science annotations\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(expert_df)} expert annotations\")\n\n    # Extract relevant columns and remove NaN values\n    citsci_df = citsci_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].dropna()\n    expert_df = expert_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].dropna()\n\n    # Get unique images from expert dataset\n    unique_images = expert_df['file_rad'].unique()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_images)} unique images in expert dataset\")\n\n    # Group by image\n    citsci_grouped = citsci_df.groupby('file_rad')\n    expert_grouped = expert_df.groupby('file_rad')\n\n    # Hyperparameter search space\n    min_samples_range = range(3, 10)  # 3-9\n    epsilon_range = range(4, 25, 2)  # 4, 6, 8, ..., 22, 24\n    shape_weight_range = np.arange(0.9, 2.0, 0.1)  # 0.9, 1.0, 1.1, ..., 1.8, 1.9\n\n    total_combinations = len(min_samples_range) * len(epsilon_range) * len(shape_weight_range)\n    print(f\"\\nTotal hyperparameter combinations: {total_combinations}\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Sort by F1 descending\n        output_data.sort(key=lambda x: (-x['F1'], x['delta']))\n\n        # Write to CSV\n        output_df = pd.DataFrame(output_data)\n        output_df.to_csv('/root/pareto_frontier.csv', index=False)\n\n        print(f\"\\nResults written to /root/pareto_frontier.csv\")\n        print(\"\\nPareto frontier:\")\n        print(output_df.to_string(index=False))\n    else:\n        print(\"No results with F1 > 0.5 found!\")","newString":"def main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n\n    # Load datasets\n    citsci_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/citsci_train.csv')\n    expert_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/expert_train.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(citsci_df)} citizen science annotations\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(expert_df)} expert annotations\")\n\n    # Extract relevant columns and remove NaN values\n    citsci_df = citsci_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].dropna()\n    expert_df = expert_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].dropna()\n\n    # Get unique images from expert dataset\n    unique_images = expert_df['file_rad'].unique()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_images)} unique images in expert dataset\")\n\n    # Group by image\n    citsci_grouped = citsci_df.groupby('file_rad')\n    expert_grouped = expert_df.groupby('file_rad')\n\n    # Hyperparameter search space\n    min_samples_range = range(3, 10)  # 3-9\n    epsilon_range = range(4, 25, 2)  # 4, 6, 8, ..., 22, 24\n    shape_weight_range = np.arange(0.9, 2.0, 0.1)  # 0.9, 1.0, 1.1, ..., 1.8, 1.9\n\n    total_combinations = len(min_samples_range) * len(epsilon_range) * len(shape_weight_range)\n    print(f\"\\nTotal hyperparameter combinations: {total_combinations}\")\n\n    # Prepare arguments for parallel processing\n    hyperparams_list = []\n    for min_samples in min_samples_range:\n        for epsilon in epsilon_range:\n            for shape_weight in shape_weight_range:\n                hyperparams_list.append(\n                    (min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, unique_images, citsci_grouped, expert_grouped)\n                )\n\n    print(f\"Starting parallel evaluation with {len(hyperparams_list)} combinations...\")\n\n    results = []\n    completed = 0\n\n    # Use ProcessPoolExecutor for parallel processing\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=None) as executor:\n        # Submit all jobs\n        future_to_params = {\n            executor.submit(evaluate_hyperparams, params): params\n            for params in hyperparams_list\n        }\n\n        # Collect results as they complete\n        for future in as_completed(future_to_params):\n            params = future_to_params[future]\n            min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight = params[0], params[1], params[2]\n\n            try:\n                result = future.result()\n                if result is not None:\n                    results.append(result)\n                    print(f\"min_samples={min_samples}, epsilon={epsilon}, shape_weight={shape_weight:.1f}: \"\n                          f\"F1={result['F1']:.5f}, delta={result['delta']:.5f}\")\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error processing {min_samples}, {epsilon}, {shape_weight}: {e}\")\n\n            completed += 1\n            if completed % 50 == 0 or completed == total_combinations:\n                print(f\"Completed {completed}/{total_combinations} combinations ({completed/total_combinations*100:.1f}%)\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nFiltered results (F1 > 0.5): {len(results)}\")\n\n    # Find Pareto frontier\n    if results:\n        # Convert to list of tuples\n        points = [(r['F1'], r['delta'], r['min_samples'], r['epsilon'], r['shape_weight'])\n                  for r in results]\n\n        pareto_points = pareto_frontier(points)\n\n        print(f\"Pareto frontier points: {len(pareto_points)}\")\n\n        # Prepare output\n        output_data = []\n        for f1, delta, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight in pareto_points:\n            output_data.append({\n                'F1': round(f1, 5),\n                'delta': round(delta, 5),\n                'min_samples': int(min_samples),\n                'epsilon': int(epsilon),\n                'shape_weight': round(shape_weight, 1)\n            })\n\n        # Sort by F1 descending\n        output_data.sort(key=lambda x: (-x['F1'], x['delta']))\n\n        # Write to CSV\n        output_df = pd.DataFrame(output_data)\n        output_df.to_csv('/root/pareto_frontier.csv', index=False)\n\n        print(f\"\\nResults written to /root/pareto_frontier.csv\")\n        print(\"\\nPareto frontier:\")\n        print(output_df.to_string(index=False))\n    else:\n        print(\"No results with F1 > 0.5 found!\")","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nMars Cloud Clustering Optimization\n\nThis script optimizes DBSCAN hyperparameters to cluster citizen science\nannotations of Mars clouds and finds the Pareto frontier of solutions\nthat balance F1 score and delta.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom sklearn.cluster import DBSCAN\nfrom sklearn.metrics import pairwise_distances\nfrom itertools import product\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n\ndef shape_weighted_distance(a, b, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute custom shape-weighted distance between two points.\n\n    Args:\n        a, b: Points as (x, y) tuples\n        shape_weight: Weight parameter w (0.9-1.9)\n\n    Returns:\n        Euclidean distance with shape weighting\n    \"\"\"\n    dx = a[0] - b[0]\n    dy = a[1] - b[1]\n    return np.sqrt((shape_weight * dx)**2 + ((2 - shape_weight) * dy)**2)\n\n\ndef custom_dbscan(X, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run DBSCAN with custom distance metric.\n\n    Args:\n        X: Array of points (n_samples, 2)\n        epsilon: Maximum distance between samples\n        min_samples: Minimum samples in neighborhood\n        shape_weight: Shape weight parameter\n\n    Returns:\n        Cluster labels\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(X) == 0:\n        return np.array([])\n\n    # Compute pairwise distances using custom metric\n    n = len(X)\n    distance_matrix = np.zeros((n, n))\n\n    for i in range(n):\n        for j in range(i+1, n):\n            dist = shape_weighted_distance(X[i], X[j], shape_weight)\n            distance_matrix[i, j] = dist\n            distance_matrix[j, i] = dist\n\n    # Use DBSCAN with precomputed distance matrix\n    dbscan = DBSCAN(eps=epsilon, min_samples=min_samples, metric='precomputed')\n    labels = dbscan.fit_predict(distance_matrix)\n\n    return labels\n\n\ndef compute_cluster_centroids(X, labels):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute centroids for each cluster (excluding noise points with label -1).\n\n    Args:\n        X: Array of points\n        labels: Cluster labels\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping cluster_id to centroid (x, y)\n    \"\"\"\n    centroids = {}\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(labels):\n        if cluster_id != -1:  # Exclude noise points\n            cluster_points = X[labels == cluster_id]\n            centroid = np.mean(cluster_points, axis=0)\n            centroids[cluster_id] = (centroid[0], centroid[1])\n\n    return centroids\n\n\ndef greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points, max_distance=100):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match cluster centroids to expert annotations using greedy matching.\n\n    Args:\n        cluster_centroids: List of cluster centroid tuples\n        expert_points: List of expert point tuples\n        max_distance: Maximum allowed matching distance\n\n    Returns:\n        List of tuples (centroid_idx, expert_idx, distance)\n    \"\"\"\n    matches = []\n    used_experts = set()\n\n    # Create list of all possible pairs with distances\n    all_pairs = []\n    for i, centroid in enumerate(cluster_centroids):\n        for j, expert in enumerate(expert_points):\n            dist = np.sqrt((centroid[0] - expert[0])**2 + (centroid[1] - expert[1])**2)\n            if dist <= max_distance:\n                all_pairs.append((dist, i, j))\n\n    # Sort by distance and greedily match\n    all_pairs.sort()\n    for dist, i, j in all_pairs:\n        if j not in used_experts:\n            matches.append((i, j, dist))\n            used_experts.add(j)\n\n    return matches\n\n\ndef compute_f1_score(num_matches, num_clusters, num_experts):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute F1 score based on matches, clusters, and experts.\n\n    Args:\n        num_matches: Number of matched pairs\n        num_clusters: Number of clusters found\n        num_experts: Number of expert annotations\n\n    Returns:\n        F1 score\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_clusters == 0 and num_experts == 0:\n        return 1.0\n    if num_clusters == 0 or num_experts == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    precision = num_matches / num_clusters if num_clusters > 0 else 0\n    recall = num_matches / num_experts if num_experts > 0 else 0\n\n    if precision + recall == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    f1 = 2 * (precision * recall) / (precision + recall)\n    return f1\n\n\ndef evaluate_image(citsci_points, expert_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate clustering performance for a single image.\n\n    Args:\n        citsci_points: Citizen science points for the image\n        expert_points: Expert annotations for the image\n        epsilon: DBSCAN epsilon parameter\n        min_samples: DBSCAN min_samples parameter\n        shape_weight: Shape weight parameter\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple (f1_score, delta)\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(citsci_points) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    # Run DBSCAN\n    labels = custom_dbscan(citsci_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight)\n\n    # Compute cluster centroids\n    centroids = compute_cluster_centroids(citsci_points, labels)\n    cluster_centroids = list(centroids.values())\n    expert_points_list = [(p[0], p[1]) for p in expert_points]\n\n    # Match clusters to experts\n    matches = greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points_list)\n\n    # Compute F1 score\n    f1 = compute_f1_score(len(matches), len(cluster_centroids), len(expert_points_list))\n\n    # Compute delta (average distance of matches)\n    if len(matches) > 0:\n        delta = np.mean([match[2] for match in matches])\n    else:\n        delta = np.nan\n\n    return f1, delta\n\n\ndef evaluate_hyperparams(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate a single hyperparameter combination across all images.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple (min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, unique_images,\n                     citsci_grouped, expert_grouped)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with results or None if F1 <= 0.5\n    \"\"\"\n    min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, unique_images, citsci_grouped, expert_grouped = args\n\n    f1_scores = []\n    deltas = []\n\n    # Evaluate each image\n    for image_id in unique_images:\n        # Get citizen science points for this image\n        if image_id in citsci_grouped.groups:\n            citsci_points = citsci_grouped.get_group(image_id)[['x', 'y']].values\n        else:\n            citsci_points = np.array([])\n\n        # Get expert points for this image\n        expert_points = expert_grouped.get_group(image_id)[['x', 'y']].values\n\n        # Evaluate\n        f1, delta = evaluate_image(\n            citsci_points, expert_points,\n            epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight\n        )\n\n        f1_scores.append(f1)\n        deltas.append(delta)\n\n    # Compute averages\n    avg_f1 = np.mean(f1_scores)\n\n    # Only return results with F1 > 0.5\n    if avg_f1 > 0.5:\n        # Average delta (excluding NaN values)\n        valid_deltas = [d for d in deltas if not np.isnan(d)]\n        avg_delta = np.mean(valid_deltas) if valid_deltas else np.nan\n\n        return {\n            'F1': avg_f1,\n            'delta': avg_delta,\n            'min_samples': min_samples,\n            'epsilon': epsilon,\n            'shape_weight': shape_weight\n        }\n\n    return None\n\n\ndef pareto_frontier(points):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find Pareto frontier from a list of points.\n\n    Args:\n        points: List of tuples (f1, delta, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of Pareto-optimal points\n    \"\"\"\n    # Sort by F1 (descending) then by delta (ascending)\n    sorted_points = sorted(points, key=lambda p: (-p[0], p[1]))\n\n    pareto_points = []\n    min_delta = float('inf')\n\n    for point in sorted_points:\n        f1, delta = point[0], point[1]\n\n        # If this point has a lower delta than any seen before at this F1 level\n        # (accounting for floating point precision)\n        if delta < min_delta - 1e-10:\n            pareto_points.append(point)\n            min_delta = delta\n\n    return pareto_points\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n\n    # Load datasets\n    citsci_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/citsci_train.csv')\n    expert_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/expert_train.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(citsci_df)} citizen science annotations\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(expert_df)} expert annotations\")\n\n    # Extract relevant columns and remove NaN values\n    citsci_df = citsci_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].dropna()\n    expert_df = expert_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].dropna()\n\n    # Get unique images from expert dataset\n    unique_images = expert_df['file_rad'].unique()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_images)} unique images in expert dataset\")\n\n    # Group by image\n    citsci_grouped = citsci_df.groupby('file_rad')\n    expert_grouped = expert_df.groupby('file_rad')\n\n    # Hyperparameter search space\n    min_samples_range = range(3, 10)  # 3-9\n    epsilon_range = range(4, 25, 2)  # 4, 6, 8, ..., 22, 24\n    shape_weight_range = np.arange(0.9, 2.0, 0.1)  # 0.9, 1.0, 1.1, ..., 1.8, 1.9\n\n    total_combinations = len(min_samples_range) * len(epsilon_range) * len(shape_weight_range)\n    print(f\"\\nTotal hyperparameter combinations: {total_combinations}\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Grid search\n    for min_samples in min_samples_range:\n        for epsilon in epsilon_range:\n            for shape_weight in shape_weight_range:\n                f1_scores = []\n                deltas = []\n\n                # Evaluate each image\n                for image_id in unique_images:\n                    # Get citizen science points for this image\n                    if image_id in citsci_grouped.groups:\n                        citsci_points = citsci_grouped.get_group(image_id)[['x', 'y']].values\n                    else:\n                        citsci_points = np.array([])\n\n                    # Compute averages\n                avg_f1 = np.mean(f1_scores)\n\n                # Only include results with F1 > 0.5\n                if avg_f1 > 0.5:\n                    # Average delta (excluding NaN values)\n                    valid_deltas = [d for d in deltas if not np.isnan(d)]\n                    avg_delta = np.mean(valid_deltas) if valid_deltas else np.nan\n\n                    results.append({\n                        'F1': avg_f1,\n                        'delta': avg_delta,\n                        'min_samples': min_samples,\n                        'epsilon': epsilon,\n                        'shape_weight': shape_weight\n                    })\n\n                    print(f\"min_samples={min_samples}, epsilon={epsilon}, shape_weight={shape_weight:.1f}: \"\n                          f\"F1={avg_f1:.5f}, delta={avg_delta:.5f}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nFiltered results (F1 > 0.5): {len(results)}\")\n\n    # Find Pareto frontier\n    if results:\n        # Convert to list of tuples\n        points = [(r['F1'], r['delta'], r['min_samples'], r['epsilon'], r['shape_weight'])\n                  for r in results]\n\n        pareto_points = pareto_frontier(points)\n\n        print(f\"Pareto frontier points: {len(pareto_points)}\")\n\n        # Prepare output\n        output_data = []\n        for f1, delta, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight in pareto_points:\n            output_data.append({\n                'F1': round(f1, 5),\n                'delta': round(delta, 5),\n                'min_samples': int(min_samples),\n                'epsilon': int(epsilon),\n                'shape_weight': round(shape_weight, 1)\n            })\n\n        # Sort by F1 descending\n        output_data.sort(key=lambda x: (-x['F1'], x['delta']))\n\n        # Write to CSV\n        output_df = pd.DataFrame(output_data)\n        output_df.to_csv('/root/pareto_frontier.csv', index=False)\n\n        print(f\"\\nResults written to /root/pareto_frontier.csv\")\n        print(\"\\nPareto frontier:\")\n        print(output_df.to_string(index=False))\n    else:\n        print(\"No results with F1 > 0.5 found!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":302,"oldLines":55,"newStart":302,"newLines":46,"lines":["     total_combinations = len(min_samples_range) * len(epsilon_range) * len(shape_weight_range)","     print(f\"\\nTotal hyperparameter combinations: {total_combinations}\")"," ","-    results = []","-","-    # Disabled for now","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nMars Cloud Clustering Optimization\n\nThis script optimizes DBSCAN hyperparameters to cluster citizen science\nannotations of Mars clouds and finds the Pareto frontier of solutions\nthat balance F1 score and delta.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom sklearn.cluster import DBSCAN\nfrom sklearn.metrics import pairwise_distances\nfrom itertools import product\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n\ndef shape_weighted_distance(a, b, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute custom shape-weighted distance between two points.\n\n    Args:\n        a, b: Points as (x, y) tuples\n        shape_weight: Weight parameter w (0.9-1.9)\n\n    Returns:\n        Euclidean distance with shape weighting\n    \"\"\"\n    dx = a[0] - b[0]\n    dy = a[1] - b[1]\n    return np.sqrt((shape_weight * dx)**2 + ((2 - shape_weight) * dy)**2)\n\n\ndef custom_dbscan(X, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run DBSCAN with custom distance metric.\n\n    Args:\n        X: Array of points (n_samples, 2)\n        epsilon: Maximum distance between samples\n        min_samples: Minimum samples in neighborhood\n        shape_weight: Shape weight parameter\n\n    Returns:\n        Cluster labels\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(X) == 0:\n        return np.array([])\n\n    # Compute pairwise distances using custom metric\n    n = len(X)\n    distance_matrix = np.zeros((n, n))\n\n    for i in range(n):\n        for j in range(i+1, n):\n            dist = shape_weighted_distance(X[i], X[j], shape_weight)\n            distance_matrix[i, j] = dist\n            distance_matrix[j, i] = dist\n\n    # Use DBSCAN with precomputed distance matrix\n    dbscan = DBSCAN(eps=epsilon, min_samples=min_samples, metric='precomputed')\n    labels = dbscan.fit_predict(distance_matrix)\n\n    return labels\n\n\ndef compute_cluster_centroids(X, labels):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute centroids for each cluster (excluding noise points with label -1).\n\n    Args:\n        X: Array of points\n        labels: Cluster labels\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping cluster_id to centroid (x, y)\n    \"\"\"\n    centroids = {}\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(labels):\n        if cluster_id != -1:  # Exclude noise points\n            cluster_points = X[labels == cluster_id]\n            centroid = np.mean(cluster_points, axis=0)\n            centroids[cluster_id] = (centroid[0], centroid[1])\n\n    return centroids\n\n\ndef greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points, max_distance=100):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match cluster centroids to expert annotations using greedy matching.\n\n    Args:\n        cluster_centroids: List of cluster centroid tuples\n        expert_points: List of expert point tuples\n        max_distance: Maximum allowed matching distance\n\n    Returns:\n        List of tuples (centroid_idx, expert_idx, distance)\n    \"\"\"\n    matches = []\n    used_experts = set()\n\n    # Create list of all possible pairs with distances\n    all_pairs = []\n    for i, centroid in enumerate(cluster_centroids):\n        for j, expert in enumerate(expert_points):\n            dist = np.sqrt((centroid[0] - expert[0])**2 + (centroid[1] - expert[1])**2)\n            if dist <= max_distance:\n                all_pairs.append((dist, i, j))\n\n    # Sort by distance and greedily match\n    all_pairs.sort()\n    for dist, i, j in all_pairs:\n        if j not in used_experts:\n            matches.append((i, j, dist))\n            used_experts.add(j)\n\n    return matches\n\n\ndef compute_f1_score(num_matches, num_clusters, num_experts):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute F1 score based on matches, clusters, and experts.\n\n    Args:\n        num_matches: Number of matched pairs\n        num_clusters: Number of clusters found\n        num_experts: Number of expert annotations\n\n    Returns:\n        F1 score\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_clusters == 0 and num_experts == 0:\n        return 1.0\n    if num_clusters == 0 or num_experts == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    precision = num_matches / num_clusters if num_clusters > 0 else 0\n    recall = num_matches / num_experts if num_experts > 0 else 0\n\n    if precision + recall == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    f1 = 2 * (precision * recall) / (precision + recall)\n    return f1\n\n\ndef evaluate_image(citsci_points, expert_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate clustering performance for a single image.\n\n    Args:\n        citsci_points: Citizen science points for the image\n        expert_points: Expert annotations for the image\n        epsilon: DBSCAN epsilon parameter\n        min_samples: DBSCAN min_samples parameter\n        shape_weight: Shape weight parameter\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple (f1_score, delta)\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(citsci_points) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    # Run DBSCAN\n    labels = custom_dbscan(citsci_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight)\n\n    # Compute cluster centroids\n    centroids = compute_cluster_centroids(citsci_points, labels)\n    cluster_centroids = list(centroids.values())\n    expert_points_list = [(p[0], p[1]) for p in expert_points]\n\n    # Match clusters to experts\n    matches = greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points_list)\n\n    # Compute F1 score\n    f1 = compute_f1_score(len(matches), len(cluster_centroids), len(expert_points_list))\n\n    # Compute delta (average distance of matches)\n    if len(matches) > 0:\n        delta = np.mean([match[2] for match in matches])\n    else:\n        delta = np.nan\n\n    return f1, delta\n\n\ndef evaluate_hyperparams(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate a single hyperparameter combination across all images.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple (min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, unique_images,\n                     citsci_grouped, expert_grouped)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with results or None if F1 <= 0.5\n    \"\"\"\n    min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, unique_images, citsci_grouped, expert_grouped = args\n\n    f1_scores = []\n    deltas = []\n\n    # Evaluate each image\n    for image_id in unique_images:\n        # Get citizen science points for this image\n        if image_id in citsci_grouped.groups:\n            citsci_points = citsci_grouped.get_group(image_id)[['x', 'y']].values\n        else:\n            citsci_points = np.array([])\n\n        # Get expert points for this image\n        expert_points = expert_grouped.get_group(image_id)[['x', 'y']].values\n\n        # Evaluate\n        f1, delta = evaluate_image(\n            citsci_points, expert_points,\n            epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight\n        )\n\n        f1_scores.append(f1)\n        deltas.append(delta)\n\n    # Compute averages\n    avg_f1 = np.mean(f1_scores)\n\n    # Only return results with F1 > 0.5\n    if avg_f1 > 0.5:\n        # Average delta (excluding NaN values)\n        valid_deltas = [d for d in deltas if not np.isnan(d)]\n        avg_delta = np.mean(valid_deltas) if valid_deltas else np.nan\n\n        return {\n            'F1': avg_f1,\n            'delta': avg_delta,\n            'min_samples': min_samples,\n            'epsilon': epsilon,\n            'shape_weight': shape_weight\n        }\n\n    return None\n\n\ndef pareto_frontier(points):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find Pareto frontier from a list of points.\n\n    Args:\n        points: List of tuples (f1, delta, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of Pareto-optimal points\n    \"\"\"\n    # Sort by F1 (descending) then by delta (ascending)\n    sorted_points = sorted(points, key=lambda p: (-p[0], p[1]))\n\n    pareto_points = []\n    min_delta = float('inf')\n\n    for point in sorted_points:\n        f1, delta = point[0], point[1]\n\n        # If this point has a lower delta than any seen before at this F1 level\n        # (accounting for floating point precision)\n        if delta < min_delta - 1e-10:\n            pareto_points.append(point)\n            min_delta = delta\n\n    return pareto_points\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n\n    # Load datasets\n    citsci_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/citsci_train.csv')\n    expert_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/expert_train.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(citsci_df)} citizen science annotations\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(expert_df)} expert annotations\")\n\n    # Extract relevant columns and remove NaN values\n    citsci_df = citsci_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].dropna()\n    expert_df = expert_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].dropna()\n\n    # Get unique images from expert dataset\n    unique_images = expert_df['file_rad'].unique()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_images)} unique images in expert dataset\")\n\n    # Group by image\n    citsci_grouped = citsci_df.groupby('file_rad')\n    expert_grouped = expert_df.groupby('file_rad')\n\n    # Hyperparameter search space\n    min_samples_range = range(3, 10)  # 3-9\n    epsilon_range = range(4, 25, 2)  # 4, 6, 8, ..., 22, 24\n    shape_weight_range = np.arange(0.9, 2.0, 0.1)  # 0.9, 1.0, 1.1, ..., 1.8, 1.9\n\n    total_combinations = len(min_samples_range) * len(epsilon_range) * len(shape_weight_range)\n    print(f\"\\nTotal hyperparameter combinations: {total_combinations}\")\n\n    # Prepare arguments for parallel processing\n    hyperparams_list = []\n    for min_samples in min_samples_range:\n        for epsilon in epsilon_range:\n            for shape_weight in shape_weight_range:\n                hyperparams_list.append(\n                    (min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, unique_images, citsci_grouped, expert_grouped)\n                )\n\n    print(f\"Starting parallel evaluation with {len(hyperparams_list)} combinations...\")\n\n    results = []\n    completed = 0\n\n    # Use ProcessPoolExecutor for parallel processing\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=None) as executor:\n        # Submit all jobs\n        future_to_params = {\n            executor.submit(evaluate_hyperparams, params): params\n            for params in hyperparams_list\n        }\n\n        # Collect results as they complete\n        for future in as_completed(future_to_params):\n            params = future_to_params[future]\n            min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight = params[0], params[1], params[2]\n\n            try:\n                result = future.result()\n                if result is not None:\n                    results.append(result)\n                    print(f\"min_samples={min_samples}, epsilon={epsilon}, shape_weight={shape_weight:.1f}: \"\n                          f\"F1={result['F1']:.5f}, delta={result['delta']:.5f}\")\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error processing {min_samples}, {epsilon}, {shape_weight}: {e}\")\n\n            completed += 1\n            if completed % 50 == 0 or completed == total_combinations:\n                print(f\"Completed {completed}/{total_combinations} combinations ({completed/total_combinations*100:.1f}%)\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nFiltered results (F1 > 0.5): {len(results)}\")\n\n    # Disabled for now\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n\ndef shape_weighted_distance(a, b, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute custom shape-weighted distance between two points.\n\n    Args:\n        a, b: Points as (x, y) tuples\n        shape_weight: Weight parameter w (0.9-1.9)\n\n    Returns:\n        Euclidean distance with shape weighting\n    \"\"\"\n    dx = a[0] - b[0]\n    dy = a[1] - b[1]\n    return np.sqrt((shape_weight * dx)**2 + ((2 - shape_weight) * dy)**2)\n\n\ndef custom_dbscan(X, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run DBSCAN with custom distance metric.\n\n    Args:\n        X: Array of points (n_samples, 2)\n        epsilon: Maximum distance between samples\n        min_samples: Minimum samples in neighborhood\n        shape_weight: Shape weight parameter\n\n    Returns:\n        Cluster labels\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(X) == 0:\n        return np.array([])\n\n    # Compute pairwise distances using custom metric\n    n = len(X)\n    distance_matrix = np.zeros((n, n))\n\n    for i in range(n):\n        for j in range(i+1, n):\n            dist = shape_weighted_distance(X[i], X[j], shape_weight)\n            distance_matrix[i, j] = dist\n            distance_matrix[j, i] = dist\n\n    # Use DBSCAN with precomputed distance matrix\n    dbscan = DBSCAN(eps=epsilon, min_samples=min_samples, metric='precomputed')\n    labels = dbscan.fit_predict(distance_matrix)\n\n    return labels\n\n\ndef compute_cluster_centroids(X, labels):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute centroids for each cluster (excluding noise points with label -1).\n\n    Args:\n        X: Array of points\n        labels: Cluster labels\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping cluster_id to centroid (x, y)\n    \"\"\"\n    centroids = {}\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(labels):\n        if cluster_id != -1:  # Exclude noise points\n            cluster_points = X[labels == cluster_id]\n            centroid = np.mean(cluster_points, axis=0)\n            centroids[cluster_id] = (centroid[0], centroid[1])\n\n    return centroids\n\n\ndef greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points, max_distance=100):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match cluster centroids to expert annotations using greedy matching.\n\n    Args:\n        cluster_centroids: List of cluster centroid tuples\n        expert_points: List of expert point tuples\n        max_distance: Maximum allowed matching distance\n\n    Returns:\n        List of tuples (centroid_idx, expert_idx, distance)\n    \"\"\"\n    matches = []\n    used_experts = set()\n\n    # Create list of all possible pairs with distances\n    all_pairs = []\n    for i, centroid in enumerate(cluster_centroids):\n        for j, expert in enumerate(expert_points):\n            dist = np.sqrt((centroid[0] - expert[0])**2 + (centroid[1] - expert[1])**2)\n            if dist <= max_distance:\n                all_pairs.append((dist, i, j))\n\n    # Sort by distance and greedily match\n    all_pairs.sort()\n    for dist, i, j in all_pairs:\n        if j not in used_experts:\n            matches.append((i, j, dist))\n            used_experts.add(j)\n\n    return matches\n\n\ndef compute_f1_score(num_matches, num_clusters, num_experts):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute F1 score based on matches, clusters, and experts.\n\n    Args:\n        num_matches: Number of matched pairs\n        num_clusters: Number of clusters found\n        num_experts: Number of expert annotations\n\n    Returns:\n        F1 score\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_clusters == 0 and num_experts == 0:\n        return 1.0\n    if num_clusters == 0 or num_experts == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    precision = num_matches / num_clusters if num_clusters > 0 else 0\n    recall = num_matches / num_experts if num_experts > 0 else 0\n\n    if precision + recall == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    f1 = 2 * (precision * recall) / (precision + recall)\n    return f1\n\n\ndef evaluate_image(citsci_points, expert_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate clustering performance for a single image.\n\n    Args:\n        citsci_points: Citizen science points for the image\n        expert_points: Expert annotations for the image\n        epsilon: DBSCAN epsilon parameter\n        min_samples: DBSCAN min_samples parameter\n        shape_weight: Shape weight parameter\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple (f1_score, delta)\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(citsci_points) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    # Run DBSCAN\n    labels = custom_dbscan(citsci_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight)\n\n    # Compute cluster centroids\n    centroids = compute_cluster_centroids(citsci_points, labels)\n    cluster_centroids = list(centroids.values())\n    expert_points_list = [(p[0], p[1]) for p in expert_points]\n\n    # Match clusters to experts\n    matches = greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points_list)\n\n    # Compute F1 score\n    f1 = compute_f1_score(len(matches), len(cluster_centroids), len(expert_points_list))\n\n    # Compute delta (average distance of matches)\n    if len(matches) > 0:\n        delta = np.mean([match[2] for match in matches])\n    else:\n        delta = np.nan\n\n    return f1, delta\n\n\ndef evaluate_hyperparams(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate a single hyperparameter combination across all images.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple (min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, unique_images,\n                     citsci_grouped, expert_grouped)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with results or None if F1 <= 0.5\n    \"\"\"\n    min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, unique_images, citsci_grouped, expert_grouped = args\n\n    f1_scores = []\n    deltas = []\n\n    # Evaluate each image\n    for image_id in unique_images:\n        # Get citizen science points for this image\n        if image_id in citsci_grouped.groups:\n            citsci_points = citsci_grouped.get_group(image_id)[['x', 'y']].values\n        else:\n            citsci_points = np.array([])\n\n        # Get expert points for this image\n        expert_points = expert_grouped.get_group(image_id)[['x', 'y']].values\n\n        # Evaluate\n        f1, delta = evaluate_image(\n            citsci_points, expert_points,\n            epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight\n        )\n\n        f1_scores.append(f1)\n        deltas.append(delta)\n\n    # Compute averages\n    avg_f1 = np.mean(f1_scores)\n\n    # Only return results with F1 > 0.5\n    if avg_f1 > 0.5:\n        # Average delta (excluding NaN values)\n        valid_deltas = [d for d in deltas if not np.isnan(d)]\n        avg_delta = np.mean(valid_deltas) if valid_deltas else np.nan\n\n        return {\n            'F1': avg_f1,\n            'delta': avg_delta,\n            'min_samples': min_samples,\n            'epsilon': epsilon,\n            'shape_weight': shape_weight\n        }\n\n    return None\n\n\ndef pareto_frontier(points):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find Pareto frontier from a list of points.\n\n    Args:\n        points: List of tuples (f1, delta, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of Pareto-optimal points\n    \"\"\"\n    # Sort by F1 (descending) then by delta (ascending)\n    sorted_points = sorted(points, key=lambda p: (-p[0], p[1]))\n\n    pareto_points = []\n    min_delta = float('inf')\n\n    for point in sorted_points:\n        f1, delta = point[0], point[1]\n\n        # If this point has a lower delta than any seen before at this F1 level\n        # (accounting for floating point precision)\n        if delta < min_delta - 1e-10:\n            pareto_points.append(point)\n            min_delta = delta\n\n    return pareto_points\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n\n    # Find Pareto frontier\n    if results:\n        # Convert to list of tuples\n        points = [(r['F1'], r['delta'], r['min_samples'], r['epsilon'], r['shape_weight'])\n                  for r in results]\n\n        pareto_points = pareto_frontier(points)\n\n        print(f\"Pareto frontier points: {len(pareto_points)}\")\n\n        # Sort by F1 descending\n        output_data.sort(key=lambda x: (-x['F1'], x['delta']))\n\n        # Write to CSV\n        output_df = pd.DataFrame(output_data)\n        output_df.to_csv('/root/pareto_frontier.csv', index=False)\n\n        print(f\"\\nResults written to /root/pareto_frontier.csv\")\n        print(\"\\nPareto frontier:\")\n        print(output_df.to_string(index=False))\n    else:\n        print(\"No results with F1 > 0.5 found!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":311,"oldLines":36,"newStart":311,"newLines":25,"lines":["                     (min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, unique_images, citsci_grouped, expert_grouped)","                 )"," ","-    print(f\"Starting parallel evaluation with {len(hyperparams_list)} combinations...\")","+    print(f\"Starting sequential evaluation with {len(hyperparams_list)} combinations...\")"," ","     results = []","     completed = 0"," ","-    # delayed(evaluate_params)(a, b) for a, b in params\n)\n\n# Filter results\nresults = [r for r in results if r is not None]\nbest = max(results, key=lambda x: x['score'])\n```\n\n## Pre-computing Shared data)\n\n# Pass shared data to each task\nresults = Parallel(n_jobs=-1)(\n    delayed(process_with_shared)(p, shared_data)\n    for p in param_list\n)\n```\n\n## Performance Tips\n\n- Only worth it Disabled for now\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')","newString":"import pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom sklearn.cluster import DBSCAN\nfrom sklearn.metrics import pairwise_distances\nfrom itertools import product\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom joblib import Parallel, delayed\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nMars Cloud Clustering Optimization\n\nThis script optimizes DBSCAN hyperparameters to cluster citizen science\nannotations of Mars clouds and finds the Pareto frontier of solutions\nthat balance F1 score and delta.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom sklearn.cluster import DBSCAN\nfrom sklearn.metrics import pairwise_distances\nfrom itertools import product\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n# from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed  # Disabled for now\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n\ndef shape_weighted_distance(a, b, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute custom shape-weighted distance between two points.\n\n    Args:\n        a, b: Points as (x, y) tuples\n        shape_weight: Weight parameter w (0.9-1.9)\n\n    Returns:\n        Euclidean distance with shape weighting\n    \"\"\"\n    dx = a[0] - b[0]\n    dy = a[1] - b[1]\n    return np.sqrt((shape_weight * dx)**2 + ((2 - shape_weight) * dy)**2)\n\n\ndef custom_dbscan(X, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run DBSCAN with custom distance metric.\n\n    Args:\n        X: Array of points (n_samples, 2)\n        epsilon: Maximum distance between samples\n        min_samples: Minimum samples in neighborhood\n        shape_weight: Shape weight parameter\n\n    Returns:\n        Cluster labels\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(X) == 0:\n        return np.array([])\n\n    # Compute pairwise distances using custom metric\n    n = len(X)\n    distance_matrix = np.zeros((n, n))\n\n    for i in range(n):\n        for j in range(i+1, n):\n            dist = shape_weighted_distance(X[i], X[j], shape_weight)\n            distance_matrix[i, j] = dist\n            distance_matrix[j, i] = dist\n\n    # Use DBSCAN with precomputed distance matrix\n    dbscan = DBSCAN(eps=epsilon, min_samples=min_samples, metric='precomputed')\n    labels = dbscan.fit_predict(distance_matrix)\n\n    return labels\n\n\ndef compute_cluster_centroids(X, labels):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute centroids for each cluster (excluding noise points with label -1).\n\n    Args:\n        X: Array of points\n        labels: Cluster labels\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping cluster_id to centroid (x, y)\n    \"\"\"\n    centroids = {}\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(labels):\n        if cluster_id != -1:  # Exclude noise points\n            cluster_points = X[labels == cluster_id]\n            centroid = np.mean(cluster_points, axis=0)\n            centroids[cluster_id] = (centroid[0], centroid[1])\n\n    return centroids\n\n\ndef greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points, max_distance=100):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match cluster centroids to expert annotations using greedy matching.\n\n    Args:\n        cluster_centroids: List of cluster centroid tuples\n        expert_points: List of expert point tuples\n        max_distance: Maximum allowed matching distance\n\n    Returns:\n        List of tuples (centroid_idx, expert_idx, distance)\n    \"\"\"\n    matches = []\n    used_experts = set()\n\n    # Create list of all possible pairs with distances\n    all_pairs = []\n    for i, centroid in enumerate(cluster_centroids):\n        for j, expert in enumerate(expert_points):\n            dist = np.sqrt((centroid[0] - expert[0])**2 + (centroid[1] - expert[1])**2)\n            if dist <= max_distance:\n                all_pairs.append((dist, i, j))\n\n    # Sort by distance and greedily match\n    all_pairs.sort()\n    for dist, i, j in all_pairs:\n        if j not in used_experts:\n            matches.append((i, j, dist))\n            used_experts.add(j)\n\n    return matches\n\n\ndef compute_f1_score(num_matches, num_clusters, num_experts):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute F1 score based on matches, clusters, and experts.\n\n    Args:\n        num_matches: Number of matched pairs\n        num_clusters: Number of clusters found\n        num_experts: Number of expert annotations\n\n    Returns:\n        F1 score\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_clusters == 0 and num_experts == 0:\n        return 1.0\n    if num_clusters == 0 or num_experts == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    precision = num_matches / num_clusters if num_clusters > 0 else 0\n    recall = num_matches / num_experts if num_experts > 0 else 0\n\n    if precision + recall == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    f1 = 2 * (precision * recall) / (precision + recall)\n    return f1\n\n\ndef evaluate_image(citsci_points, expert_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate clustering performance for a single image.\n\n    Args:\n        citsci_points: Citizen science points for the image\n        expert_points: Expert annotations for the image\n        epsilon: DBSCAN epsilon parameter\n        min_samples: DBSCAN min_samples parameter\n        shape_weight: Shape weight parameter\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple (f1_score, delta)\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(citsci_points) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    # Run DBSCAN\n    labels = custom_dbscan(citsci_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight)\n\n    # Compute cluster centroids\n    centroids = compute_cluster_centroids(citsci_points, labels)\n    cluster_centroids = list(centroids.values())\n    expert_points_list = [(p[0], p[1]) for p in expert_points]\n\n    # Match clusters to experts\n    matches = greedy_matching(cluster_centroids, expert_points_list)\n\n    # Compute F1 score\n    f1 = compute_f1_score(len(matches), len(cluster_centroids), len(expert_points_list))\n\n    # Compute delta (average distance of matches)\n    if len(matches) > 0:\n        delta = np.mean([match[2] for match in matches])\n    else:\n        delta = np.nan\n\n    return f1, delta\n\n\ndef evaluate_hyperparams(args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate a single hyperparameter combination across all images.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple (min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, unique_images,\n                     citsci_grouped, expert_grouped)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with results or None if F1 <= 0.5\n    \"\"\"\n    min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, unique_images, citsci_grouped, expert_grouped = args\n\n    f1_scores = []\n    deltas = []\n\n    # Evaluate each image\n    for image_id in unique_images:\n        # Get citizen science points for this image\n        if image_id in citsci_grouped.groups:\n            citsci_points = citsci_grouped.get_group(image_id)[['x', 'y']].values\n        else:\n            citsci_points = np.array([])\n\n        # Get expert points for this image\n        expert_points = expert_grouped.get_group(image_id)[['x', 'y']].values\n\n        # Evaluate\n        f1, delta = evaluate_image(\n            citsci_points, expert_points,\n            epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight\n        )\n\n        f1_scores.append(f1)\n        deltas.append(delta)\n\n    # Compute averages\n    avg_f1 = np.mean(f1_scores)\n\n    # Only return results with F1 > 0.5\n    if avg_f1 > 0.5:\n        # Average delta (excluding NaN values)\n        valid_deltas = [d for d in deltas if not np.isnan(d)]\n        avg_delta = np.mean(valid_deltas) if valid_deltas else np.nan\n\n        return {\n            'F1': avg_f1,\n            'delta': avg_delta,\n            'min_samples': min_samples,\n            'epsilon': epsilon,\n            'shape_weight': shape_weight\n        }\n\n    return None\n\n\ndef pareto_frontier(points):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find Pareto frontier from a list of points.\n\n    Args:\n        points: List of tuples (f1, delta, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of Pareto-optimal points\n    \"\"\"\n    # Sort by F1 (descending) then by delta (ascending)\n    sorted_points = sorted(points, key=lambda p: (-p[0], p[1]))\n\n    pareto_points = []\n    min_delta = float('inf')\n\n    for point in sorted_points:\n        f1, delta = point[0], point[1]\n\n        # If this point has a lower delta than any seen before at this F1 level\n        # (accounting for floating point precision)\n        if delta < min_delta - 1e-10:\n            pareto_points.append(point)\n            min_delta = delta\n\n    return pareto_points\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n\n    # Load datasets\n    citsci_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/citsci_train.csv')\n    expert_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/expert_train.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(citsci_df)} citizen science annotations\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(expert_df)} expert annotations\")\n\n    # Extract relevant columns and remove NaN values\n    citsci_df = citsci_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].dropna()\n    expert_df = expert_df[['file_rad', 'x', 'y']].dropna()\n\n    # Get unique images from expert dataset\n    unique_images = expert_df['file_rad'].unique()\n    print(f\"Found {len(unique_images)} unique images in expert dataset\")\n\n    # Group by image\n    citsci_grouped = citsci_df.groupby('file_rad')\n    expert_grouped = expert_df.groupby('file_rad')\n\n    # Hyperparameter search space\n    min_samples_range = range(3, 10)  # 3-9\n    epsilon_range = range(4, 25, 2)  # 4, 6, 8, ..., 22, 24\n    shape_weight_range = np.arange(0.9, 2.0, 0.1)  # 0.9, 1.0, 1.1, ..., 1.8, 1.9\n\n    total_combinations = len(min_samples_range) * len(epsilon_range) * len(shape_weight_range)\n    print(f\"\\nTotal hyperparameter combinations: {total_combinations}\")\n\n    # Prepare arguments for parallel processing\n    hyperparams_list = []\n    for min_samples in min_samples_range:\n        for epsilon in epsilon_range:\n            for shape_weight in shape_weight_range:\n                hyperparams_list.append(\n                    (min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, unique_images, citsci_grouped, expert_grouped)\n                )\n\n    print(f\"Starting sequential evaluation with {len(hyperparams_list)} combinations...\")\n\n    results = []\n    completed = 0\n\n    # Sequential evaluation\n    for params in hyperparams_list:\n        min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, unique_images, citsci_grouped, expert_grouped = params\n\n        result = evaluate_hyperparams(params)\n\n        if result is not None:\n            results.append(result)\n            print(f\"min_samples={min_samples}, epsilon={epsilon}, shape_weight={shape_weight:.1f}: \"\n                  f\"F1={result['F1']:.5f}, delta={result['delta']:.5f}\")\n\n        completed += 1\n        if completed % 10 == 0 or completed == total_combinations:\n            print(f\"Completed {completed}/{total_combinations} combinations ({completed/total_combinations*100:.1f}%)\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nFiltered results (F1 > 0.5): {len(results)}\")\n\n    # Find Pareto frontier\n    if results:\n        # Convert to list of tuples\n        points = [(r['F1'], r['delta'], r['min_samples'], r['epsilon'], r['shape_weight'])\n                  for r in results]\n\n        pareto_points = pareto_frontier(points)\n\n        print(f\"Pareto frontier points: {len(pareto_points)}\")\n\n        # Prepare output\n        output_data = []\n        for f1, delta, min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight in pareto_points:\n            output_data.append({\n                'F1': round(f1, 5),\n                'delta': round(delta, 5),\n                'min_samples': int(min_samples),\n                'epsilon': int(epsilon),\n                'shape_weight': round(shape_weight, 1)\n            })\n\n        # Sort by F1 descending\n        output_data.sort(key=lambda x: (-x['F1'], x['delta']))\n\n        # Write to CSV\n        output_df = pd.DataFrame(output_data)\n        output_df.to_csv('/root/pareto_frontier.csv', index=False)\n\n        print(f\"\\nResults written to /root/pareto_frontier.csv\")\n        print(\"\\nPareto frontier:\")\n        print(output_df.to_string(index=False))\n    else:\n        print(\"No results with F1 > 0.5 found!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":13,"oldLines":7,"newStart":13,"newLines":7,"lines":[" from sklearn.metrics import pairwise_distances"," from itertools import product"," from collections import defaultdict","-# from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed  #
